Hi,

We’ve crossed paths around the office a few times, and I’ve been meaning to say hello. I recently joined a dating site and thought it might be easier to connect there outside of work.

If you’re open to it, here’s my profile: https://displayinsights.online/profile/12331/
You’ll need to sign up to view it.

No pressure at all—just an invitation to chat in a space that isn’t tied to corporate email. 

Either way, wishing you a great week.

Best,
A colleague
```

```
Urgent Request to Review Suspicious Spending Patterns, Fraud Report

Our monitoring systems flagged transactions that appear inconsistent with your typical spending behavior. While we cannot disclose specific details for security reasons, this may include unexpected purchases, geographic irregularities, or high-frequency activity:

To protect your account from potential fraud, we have temporarily locked it as a precautionary measure and immediate action is required.

Review your account Now

Check your transaction history for any unauthorized charges. If you recognize suspicious activity, report it immediately.
If fraudulent transactions are confirmed, Capital One will issue a provisional credit and investigate further. You will not be held responsible for unauthorized charges.

Highlights of your changes fromSeptember 19, 2025 to October 17, 2025

Payment history9/9
 account payments made on time

To lenders, your history of on-time payments indicates whether you’ll make payments on time in the future.

New accounts0
 new accounts

To lenders, opening too many new accounts in a short window of time could point to credit problems.

Credit usage30
% ? 27% of total credit used

See how much credit you are using compared to your total credit—the lower your credit usage, the better.

See your score

Check your credit score and other key factors with the CreditWise app.

Download the CreditWise mobile app today.

About this message
The CreditWise Simulator provides an estimate of your score change and does not guarantee how your score may change.Web access is needed to use the mobile app. Check with your service provider for details on specific fees and charges.

Important information from Capital One

Contact us  |  
Privacy
  |  
Help prevent fraud
To ensure delivery, add capitalone@notification.capitalone.com to your address book.
This email was sent to jw@allermetrix.com 
and contains information directly related to your CreditWise account (including CreditWise alerts) and other services to which you have subscribed. If you no longer want to receive these emails, unfortunately you’ll have to unenroll from CreditWise
.
Where does my CreditWise score come from?
The credit score provided in CreditWise is a FICO® Score 8 based on TransUnion® data. The FICO Score 8 gives you a good sense of your credit health, but it may not be the same score model used by your lender or creditor. The availability of the CreditWise tool and certain features in the tool depends on our ability to obtain your credit history from TransUnion and whether you have sufficient credit history to generate a FICO Score 8. Some monitoring and alerts may not be available to 
you if the information you enter at enrollment does not match the information in your credit file at (or you do not have a file at) one or more consumer reporting agencies.
Capital One does not provide, endorse or guarantee any third-party product, service, information or recommendation listed above. The third parties listed are not affiliated with Capital One and are solely responsible for their products and services. All trademarks are the property of their respective owners.
Please do not reply to this message, as this email inbox is not monitored. 

To contact us, visit www.capitalone.com/help-center/contact-us.
Products and services are offered by Capital One, N.A.

```
Hello,



I'm writing to you because I believe a custom mobile app could be a
game-changer for your business.
 
Our team has a lot of experience building high-quality iOS and Android
applications. With over 200 global projects, we've helped businesses just
like yours create new revenue streams and build stronger customer loyalty
through thoughtful UI/UX design and seamless API integration.
 
We'd love to hear about your goals. Could you share some of your
requirements so we can discuss your vision?

 

We are happy to connect via email or share your WhatsApp.
 
Best Regards,
Elsie


P.S.: If you'd prefer not to receive further emails from me, simply reply
with "NO" in the subject line.

Greetings,

We are a team of seasoned financial managers actively seeking to 
collaborate with project owners who present well-structured 
proposals with clear and compelling return on investment 
potential. Our mission is to strategically support and partner 
with promising ventures, driving long-term financial growth and 
sustainable success.

If your project aligns with these criteria, we would be delighted 
to explore the opportunity of working together. 

Should your proposal demonstrate strong potential, we would be 
glad to arrange a Zoom meeting at your convenience to discuss the 
details further.

Note: Our loan comes with a flexible repayment plan. Competitive 
rate with no hidden fees. The loan is tailored to your needs.

Regards,

Mr. Alexandria Kurt

```

Dear Sir/Madam 
How are you doing? Are you an entrepreneur/business owner or chief executive officer seeking capital for your business growth or expansion?
I am contacting you to know if you are open to investors into your company as we are currently providing financial support to companies and individuals for business and project expansion.
Our company will be willing to partner with you for your business growth.
Reply for further discussions if interested with your business plan or executive summary.
Ignore if not interested.
Best regards, Erich KellerInvestment Broker | Project Finance Consultant.
```

## Email Content

```
Hello,

Thank you for contacting the Imago Dei Fund! Your message is important to us. Please note that this inbox is regularly monitored, and while we strive to respond quickly, the high volume of inquiries may cause some delays. We will respond to inquiries as soon as possible.

In the meantime, feel free to explore our website at imagodeifund.org and sign up for our newsletter for more information about our grantmaking and initiatives. Thank you for your patience and understanding.

With gratitude,
Imago Dei Fund

---

Hello,

Thank you for contacting the Imago Dei Fund! Your message is important to us. Please note that this inbox is regularly monitored, and while we strive to respond quickly, the high volume of inquiries may cause some delays. We will respond to inquiries as soon as possible.

In the meantime, feel free to explore our website and sign up for our newsletter for more information about our grantmaking and initiatives. Thank you for your patience and understanding.

With gratitude,
Imago Dei Fund
```

Happy playing!
Scott

Health Edition


Breaking News

Bill Gates Invests $500 Million in Natural "Memory Restoration" Protocol

claiming it could end the Alzheimer's crisis.

Updated 10:45 AM ET, Thu January 22, 2026


Bill Gates Interview


Watch Report


(CNN) — A controversial new medical report has just been leaked, revealing  
a massive investment that is shaking the pharmaceutical industry to its  
core.

This natural protocol, now backed by tech billionaires including Bill

in a matter of weeks.

While Big Pharma is reportedly fighting to keep this information off the

their mental clarity.

below to see the proof before the video is taken down.

Hei,

Kiitos yhteydenotostasi. Tämä on automaattivastaus.

Olemme vastaanottaneet viestisi ja vastaamme siihen mahdollisimman pian. Jos haluat jatkaa keskustelua tästä asiasta, vastaathan tähän viestiin ja säilytät viestin otsikon ennallaan. Tämä auttaa meitä käsittelemään asiasi nopeammin.

Tiesithän, että voit laittaa liittymishakemuksia, omistajanvaihtoilmoituksia, laskutusosoitteen muutoksia ja vesimittarilukemailmoituksia vireille myös kotisivujemme kautta [1]https://www.kuopionvesi.fi/asioi-verkossa/.

Palvelunumeromme ovat:

· Laskutus, vesimittarilukemat ja omistajanvaihdokset 029 371 2550 (puhelinpalveluaika maanantai - torstai klo 10 - 13)

· Tekninen asiakasneuvonta, liittymisasiat 029 371 2551 (puhelinpalveluaika tiistai ja torstai klo 10 - 13)

Laskujemme maksujärjestelyt ja perinnän hoitaa Ropo Suomi Oy. Ropolla on käytössään [2]MyRopo palvelu, jossa kuluttaja- ja yritysasiakkaamme voivat mm. maksaa laskun, tallentaa laskukopion sekä tehdä maksujärjestelyjä 24/7. MyRopossa on myös Chat-palvelu. Ropo Oy:n puhelinpalvelu toimii numerossa 09 4246 1314 seuraavasti: kuluttaja-asiakkaiden maksuneuvonta ma–pe klo 8–20, yritysasiakkaiden puhelinpalveluaika ma–pe klo 8–17.

Vika- ja häiriötilanteissa pyydämme ottamaan yhteyttä puhelimitse:

· Vesi- ja jätevesiverkoston sekä jätevedenpuhdistamoiden ja pumppaamoiden vika ja häiriöilmoitukset 029 371 2553

· Vedenlaatuhäiriöt 029 371 2554

Ystävällisin terveisin

KUOPION VESI OY
Asiakaspalvelu
asiakaspalvelu[at]kuopionvesi.fi
[3]www.kuopionvesi.fi

Hi,

 

Do you need a new Website or want to improve your current one? I am a
graphic designer / web developer from India, ready to provide customized
solutions for your business.

 

I'd love to share my portfolio and work with you to create something that
fits your needs perfectly. Share your ideas, or let me know your phone
number for quick communication.

 

Looking forward to connecting with you!

 

Best regards,

Shakti,

 

P.S.: If you'd prefer not to receive further emails from me, simply reply
with "NO" in the subject line.

Good day Sir/madam,I am pleased to inform you that CPC is currently offering long-term debt financing solutions designed to support project development and business expansion initiatives.Our facilities are available at a highly competitive fixed interest rate of 3% per annum, with tenors ranging from 10 to 25 years, and include a 24-month grace period to support smooth project execution and cash-flow stability.We also welcome collaboration with certified introducers and brokers. Successful intermediaries who connect our firm with qualified project owners are entitled to a 2% commission on the total loan amount upon closing.Please feel free to contact me directly should you require additional information or wish to explore this opportunity further.Best Regards,Erich KellerInvestment & Loan Consultant

```
Neue Anfrage

Guten Tag,

wir haben Ihre Anfrage erhalten und werden uns so schnell wie mglich bei Ihnen melden.

Whrend Sie auf unsere Antwort warten, knnen Sie sich auch in unserem Hilfebereich umsehen oder sich mit weiteren Doctolib Nutzer:Innen in unserer Doctolib Community austauschen und Produktideen einreichen.

Sie mchten zuknftig schnellere Antworten und mehr Transparenz bezglich Ihrer Anfragen?

Dann kontaktieren Sie uns einfach direkt ber Ihren Kalender, indem Sie oben rechts auf Support kontaktieren klicken. Sie knnen darber einen Anruf anfordern, eine schriftliche Anfrage stellen und den Status Ihrer Anfrage anschlieend besser nachvollziehen. Mit dieser Funktion mchten wir Ihnen den Zugang zu unserem Kundenservice erleichtern.

Viele Gre,

Ihr Doctolib-Team

ref:!00D1i0981I.!500Tn0Z9ytn:ref
